# Triage of the open pkp/pkp-lib issues

Every issue open on [pkp/pkp-lib](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues) on 2026-11-05 (1,099), classified by what it is, whatever its labels say, and, for bugs, whether the fault is still on `main`. Round 1 is a code read against the tips of `main` and `stable-3_5_0` of 2026-11-05; a bug that is still open, not already tracked on pkp-e2e and reproducible from the default dataset is walked in round 2 and, when the walk confirms it, filed on pkp-e2e as an issue report (MAINTENANCE "Issue reports"). The **pkp-e2e** column names the tracking issue.

Columns: **Kind** bug, feature, enhancement, task, docs, question, discussion, translation. **Status** for a bug: open (still on `main`), fixed, not-a-bug, obsolete, duplicate, cannot-verify; for the rest: open, done, obsolete, duplicate. **3.5** whether `stable-3_5_0` still has an open bug. **Conf.** how sure the code read is. A security-shaped issue says only that it was routed to the private review.

Triaged: 1099 of 1099.

| Kind | Count |
|---|---|
| bug | 394 |
| enhancement | 306 |
| feature | 196 |
| task | 140 |
| discussion | 46 |
| docs | 12 |
| translation | 4 |
| question | 1 |

| Bug status | Count |
|---|---|
| open | 244 |
| fixed | 59 |
| obsolete | 39 |
| cannot-verify | 33 |
| not-a-bug | 17 |
| duplicate | 2 |

Round 2 queue: 124 open bugs not yet tracked on pkp-e2e ("to file" below), 117 findings once same-fault issues are grouped; by the code read's severity guess: low 70, medium 46, high 1. Each is walked on `main` and 3.5 before it is filed; one the walk does not reproduce is recorded here instead.

| Issue | Title | Kind | Status | 3.5 | Apps | Conf. | pkp-e2e | Conclusion |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [#13723](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13723) | [Sessions] Deleting a session can fail with a database deadlock | bug | obsolete | no | all | high |  | A deadlock in SessionDAO::deleteById() fails a logout/login on 3.3 (and 3.4). main and stable-3_5_0 have no SessionDAO any more (sessions use Laravel's database driver, classes/core/PKPSessionServiceProvider.php), so the code it is about is gone; the fix PRs target 3.3/3.4 only (#13724, #13725, open). |
| [#13719](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13719) | [Queries] Submitting an edit of a note that was deleted returns HTTP 500 | bug | obsolete | yes | all | high |  | Editing a query note deleted meanwhile crashes QueryNoteForm::execute() on a null note. On main the legacy discussion grids and controllers/grid/queries were removed (pkp-lib 1b200a5549, #12242), so the code is gone; stable-3_5_0 still has controllers/grid/queries/form/QueryNoteForm.php execute() calling Note::find() and using the result unchecked. Fix PRs for 3.3/3.4/3.5 open (#13720-#13722). |
| [#13715](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13715) | "Request Author Response" refuses an empty subject or message with "An unexpected error has occurred" | bug | open | n/a | ojs,omp | high | pkp-e2e#815 | Request Author Response page posts without expectValidationError and never passes errors to Composer, so a 422 shows the generic error dialog (ui-library RequestReviewRoundAuthorResponse.vue). Filed from pkp-e2e#815; fix PRs ui-library#1042 and ojs#6026 still open, so still on main. |
| [#13714](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13714) | [Body text editor] \| "Send to Editor" of docx file results in "import failed" | bug | fixed | n/a | ojs | high |  | Send to Text Editor failed loading WebAssembly assets resolved against the wrong base URL. Fixed by ui-library cee127f0 (ui-library#1044, the submodule pointer on ojs main) and ojs 5841a482eb (ojs#6027, self-hosted MathJax fonts), both merged. |
| [#13713](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13713) | [Site pages] \| Changing language on site-level pages leads to a 404 when base_url[index] is set | bug | open | yes | all | medium | to file | With base_url[index] set, PKPRouter::_urlGetBaseAndContext() returns the override with a null context, so PKPPageRouter::url() builds site pages without a context/locale segment that PKPPageRouter::_setLocale() expects; its rewrite of the source path then inserts the new locale beside the old one (/index/fr_CA/en/...) and the page 404s. No fix commit on main or 3.5; code read only, exact path of the extra segment not walked. |
| [#13708](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13708) | [OJS/OMP 3.5] \| Upgrade - upgrade.xml, Static Pages are moved to Custom Pages on every upgrade | bug | open | yes | ojs,omp | high | to file | dbscripts/xml/upgrade.xml (OJS line 24, OMP line 23) still calls migrateStaticPagesToNavigationMenuItems at top level, outside any <upgrade> block, so Installer::parseInstallNodes() runs it on every upgrade and moves Static Pages into Custom Page menu items. Same on main and stable-3_5_0; fix PRs (ojs#6018/#6019, omp#2547/#2548, pkp-lib#13710) are open. |
| [#13702](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13702) | [API] OpenAPI spec generated by buildSwagger.php is invalid | bug | open | yes | all | medium | to file | lib/pkp/tools/buildSwagger.php copies schema properties verbatim (apiSummary, validation, ... leak), encodes empty properties as [] and nests $ref props under properties (lines ~81-151), so the generated Swagger 2.0 document fails linting/viewers. No fix on main. |
| [#13700](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13700) | grobidMetadataExtractor: "Attempt to read property nodeValue on null" for authors without forename or surname | bug | open | unknown | plugin:grobidMetadataExtractor | high |  | pkp/grobidMetadataExtractor GrobidMetadataExtractorPlugin.php lines 222-223 still read ->item(0)->nodeValue without a null check, so a Grobid author lacking forename/surname logs a PHP warning. Plugin not bundled and needs a Grobid service, so not reproducible from the default dataset. |
| [#13699](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13699) | Undefined array key warning for submission files uploaded by disabled users | bug | open | yes | all | high | to file | submissionFile/maps/Schema.php getUploaderUsernames() uses the user Collector whose status defaults to STATUS_ACTIVE, and mapByProperties() reads $uploaderUsernames[$userId] unguarded (main line 230, 3.5 line 216), so files uploaded by a disabled user give an Undefined array key warning and no uploader username. No fix on either branch. |
| [#13693](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13693) | installPluginVersion.php tool: foreach() warning in Plugin::installEmailTemplates() | bug | open | yes | all | high | to file | Plugin::installEmailTemplates() loops over $installer->installedLocales, which is null when called from tools/installPluginVersion.php, giving a foreach() warning; templates still install via the locale fallback (per bozana). Code unchanged on main and 3.5; fix PRs pkp-lib#13696/#13703/#13704/#13705/#13709 open. |
| [#13689](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13689) | Web Feed RSS 2.0 returns 500 (date() TypeError) when the journal has no published content | bug | open | yes | all | high | to file | webFeed templates/rss2.tpl (OJS/OMP/OPS, main and 3.5) captures {$latestDate\|strtotime} into a string and passes it to date(); with no published content WebFeedGatewayPlugin leaves latestDate null, so date() gets '' and PHP 8 throws a TypeError (HTTP 500). Unchanged on main. |
| [#13682](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13682) | Article statistics table shows HTML entities in titles (e.g. L&#039;art) | bug | open | yes | ojs,omp,ops | medium | to file | The publication map returns fullTitle as stored HTML (publication/maps/Schema.php: getFullTitles('html')) and templates/stats/publications.tpl renders it with text interpolation {{ localize(row.publication.fullTitle) }}, so stored entities and markup (&amp;, &#039;, <i>) show literally in Statistics > Articles. Same on main and 3.5. Distinct from pkp-e2e#649 (the Articles Report CSV). |
| [#13680](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/13680) | DataCite export fails for supplementary file galleys with a subject | bug | open | yes | ojs | high | pkp-e2e#1083 | DataciteXmlFilter::process() maps every subject through $s['name'], but a supplementary file's subject is a plain string, so a galley file with a subject throws a TypeError on export/deposit. Still in plugins/generic/datacite/filter/DataciteXmlFilter.php on main (line 220) and stable-3_5_0 (line 208); tracked as pkp-e2e#1083 (with pkp-lib#9170 for main). |

| [#12899](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/12899) | Fix ROR ID regex: [^ILOU] doesn't match the ROR identifier spec | bug | fixed | no | all | high |  | Fixed by pkp-lib#13751 (main, merged 2026-11-09 as 8888001f0a) and #13752 (3.5, 03d5890f72), ore #13771 (2026-11-10, 6722bea1ed; OJS ore aa4b02810f points at it, walked there): every ROR check uses 0[a-hj-km-np-tv-z0-9]{6}. PR-reviewed 2026-11-09; walked at the merge 2026-11-10 (main pinned for OJS and OPS, whose pointers lag; OMP main and 3.5 at its pointer; 3.5 OJS pinned; the OJS bump PRs ojs#6053 and #6054 are not to be merged, the pointers move with other issues' PRs, bozana 2026-11-10). U66 A5 / U41 fn d cite it. Before: The negated class [^ILOU] is still in schemas/affiliation.json, institution.json, ror.json, funder.json, api/v1/rors/PKPRorController.php:194 and I7135_CreateNewRorRegistryCacheTables::normalizeRor on main (and the first three on 3.5), so a ROR with '/', '-', '.' or lowercase i/l/o/u passes validation. pkp-e2e specs U66/U41 cite the regex but record no finding on it. |

| [#12520](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/12520) | [ROR Affiliations] \| Submission Workflow - Contributor Form, when editing a contributor with an existing ROR affiliation and submission locale has a 3-letter lang code there is an error blocking the user | bug | fixed | no | all | high |  | Fixed by pkp-lib#13743 (main, merged 2026-11-09 as 8381b1efde) and #13745 (3.5, 5bd26ee348): Ror::getNameForLocale() falls back to the registry's display name for a locale with no ISO 639-1 code. PR-reviewed 2026-11-06 (U41 Rule 16, footnote p); walked at the merge 2026-11-10 on main (OMP's pointer carries it; OJS and OPS not yet, pinned) and on 3.5 at the three apps' pointers. Before: lib/pkp classes/affiliation/Affiliation.php getAffiliationName(null, $locales) maps each submission locale through LocaleConversion::getIso1FromLocale(), which returns null for locales with no ISO 639-1 code (ckb, kab, fil...); Ror::getName(null) then returns the whole names array, so that locale's affiliation 'name' is an array and the contributor form cannot be saved. Same code on 3.5; no fix commit or PR. |

| [#9493](https://github.com/pkp/pkp-lib/issues/9493) | Empty mails are sent after a new submission, encoding problem? | bug | open | yes | all | medium | to file | When a context's only languages lack an email's translation, the automatic emails crash. EmailTemplate DAO::installEmailTemplateLocaleData stores '' for missing translations, and DAO::fromRow loads default text only for the context's supportedLocales, so getLocalizedData('body') returns null. Mailer::renderView then throws 'View must be instance of Htmlable or a string, null is given'. This happens in AssignEditors (SUBMISSION_NEEDS_EDITOR) and PKPReviewerReviewStep3Form (REVIEW_COMPLETE), which have no fallback, unlike saveForLater. Italian (lib/pkp locale/it) still lacks 23 of the 66 OJS template bodies on main and 3.5, SUBMISSION_NEEDS_EDITOR and REVIEW_COMPLETE among them. pkp-lib#9444 only made Mailable::body() accept null. |
